"""Fresh exact local CIM immediately before a single selected control finite."""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse,json,os,runpy,subprocess,sys,time,traceback
N=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;R=N/'reports'
def put(p,v):
    with p.open('x',encoding='utf8') as f:json.dump(v,f,indent=2,allow_nan=False)
def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser();ap.add_argument('phase',choices=('activation','first','normal'));a=ap.parse_args()
    selected={'activation':N/'run_ONE178_activation_exact_fresh_CIM_V2_once.py','first':N/'observe_ONE178_first_NEW_native_policy_once.py','normal':N/'collect_ONE178_original_formal_cost_normal900_V2_fullreports_once.py'}[a.phase]
    marker=R/('SELECTED178_'+a.phase.upper()+'_LOCAL_DRIVER_CONSUMED.json');assert not marker.exists()
    query=r'''Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name='python.exe' OR Name='ssh.exe'" | Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -match 'run_ONE178_selected_exact|install_NATIVE178|run_ONE178_activation|observe_ONE178_first|collect_ONE178_original|run_ROOT172_latest|run_ROOT171_secure|recover_NEW169_|run_ROOT167_secure|install_NATIVE165|run_ONE165_activation|observe_ONE165_first|collect_ONE165_original|collect_E3_source210_TERMINAL' } | Select-Object Name,ProcessId,CreationDate,ExecutablePath,CommandLine | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5 -Compress'''
    p=subprocess.run(['pwsh.exe','-NoProfile','-Command',query],capture_output=True,text=True,encoding='utf8',check=True)
    rows=json.loads(p.stdout) if p.stdout.strip() else [];rows=rows if isinstance(rows,list) else [rows];other=[x for x in rows if x['ProcessId']!=os.getpid()]
    now=time.time();cim=dict(epoch=now,self_PID=os.getpid(),self_exe=sys.executable,self_argv=sys.argv,exact_known_control_finite_matches=other,all_known_finites=rows)
    cp=R/('LOCAL_EXACT_CIM_BEFORE178_'+a.phase.upper()+'_'+str(time.time_ns())+'.json');put(cp,cim)
    assert not other and time.time()-now<=30
    put(marker,dict(epoch=time.time(),selected_entry=str(selected),phase=a.phase,new_global_serial_lease='ROOT178_EXCLUSIVE',no_other_control_finite=True,original_caps_or_guard_relaxation=False))
    old=sys.argv;sys.argv=[str(selected),'--fresh-local-cim-json',str(cp)]
    try:runpy.run_path(str(selected),run_name='__main__')
    finally:sys.argv=old
if __name__=='__main__':
    try:main()
    except BaseException:
        put(R/('SELECTED178_FINITE_ORIGINAL_ERROR_'+str(time.time_ns())+'.json'),dict(epoch=time.time(),error=traceback.format_exc(),no_auto_retry_or_second_watcher=True));raise

"""One no-step native WM32 derivative/memory readback on already-loaded WM.

Held states/actions below are a numerical fixture, never training transitions.
No checkpoint/goal/raw reader, actor/critic, optimizer construction or stepping.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import ast,hashlib,types,time
import torch

def check(mdp,wm,learner_source,initial_s,initial_xi,*,batch,expected_args32):
    from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.geometry import unflatten_state
    assert expected_args32['wm_window']==32 and expected_args32['horizon']==32
    assert batch in (4,1024) and (batch==4 or initial_s.device.type=='cuda')
    assert initial_s.shape[-1]==127 and initial_xi.shape[-1]==31
    raw=Path(learner_source).read_bytes();tree=ast.parse(raw)
    cls=next(x for x in tree.body if isinstance(x,ast.ClassDef) and x.name=='SuperMimicLearner')
    methods=[x for x in cls.body if isinstance(x,ast.FunctionDef) and x.name in ('_wm_rollout','_wm_err')]
    assert len(methods)==2
    namespace=dict(torch=torch,State=tuple,unflatten_state=unflatten_state)
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=methods,type_ignores=[]),str(learner_source),'exec'),namespace)
    device=initial_s.device
    err_floor=torch.cat((torch.full((3,),3e-3),torch.full((3,),5e-3),torch.full((3,),2e-2),torch.full((3,),5e-2),torch.full((30,),3e-3),torch.full((30,),5e-2),torch.full((54,),1e-3))).to(device)
    cfg=types.SimpleNamespace(**expected_args32)
    instance=types.SimpleNamespace(wm=wm,mdp=mdp,cfg=cfg,err_floor=err_floor)
    before={k:v.detach().clone() for k,v in wm.state_dict().items()}
    parameters=tuple(wm.parameters());flags=[p.requires_grad for p in parameters]
    assert all(p.grad is None for p in parameters),'No preexisting gradients before readback'
    rng=torch.random.get_rng_state().clone()
    cuda_rng=torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all() if device.type=='cuda' else None
    started=time.time()
    try:
        for p in parameters:p.requires_grad_(True)
        ids=torch.arange(batch,device=device)%initial_s.shape[0]
        s=initial_s[ids].detach()[:,None,:].expand(-1,32,-1).contiguous()
        xi=initial_xi[ids].detach()[:,None,:].expand(-1,32,-1).contiguous()
        actions=s.new_zeros(batch,32,6);mask=s.new_ones(batch,32)
        memory=None
        if device.type=='cuda':
            torch.cuda.synchronize();torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
            memory=dict(allocated_before=torch.cuda.memory_allocated(),reserved_before=torch.cuda.memory_reserved())
        with torch.inference_mode(False),torch.enable_grad():
            predicted=namespace['_wm_rollout'](instance,s,actions,xi)
            loss,last_error=namespace['_wm_err'](instance,predicted,s,mask)
            assert all(torch.isfinite(x).all() for x in predicted) and torch.isfinite(loss) and torch.isfinite(last_error).all()
            gradients=torch.autograd.grad(loss,parameters,allow_unused=False)
            assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() for g in gradients)
            norm=torch.sqrt(sum(g.detach().square().sum() for g in gradients))
            assert torch.isfinite(norm) and norm>0
        if device.type=='cuda':
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            memory.update(peak_allocated=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated(),peak_reserved=torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved(),device_total=torch.cuda.get_device_properties(device).total_memory)
        assert all(torch.equal(v,wm.state_dict()[k]) for k,v in before.items())
        assert all(p.grad is None for p in parameters)
        assert torch.equal(rng,torch.random.get_rng_state())
        if cuda_rng is not None:assert all(torch.equal(a,b) for a,b in zip(cuda_rng,torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all()))
        return dict(passed=True,source_SHA=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),horizon32=True,training_window32=True,batch=batch,device=str(device),native_rollout_and_error_AST_used=True,base_loss_reduction='sumtime/batch; no division before backward',loss_sumtime_perbatch=float(loss.detach()),log_loss_perstep=float(loss.detach()/32),WM_gradient_L2_before_original_clip1=float(norm),nonzero_gradient_tensors=sum(bool((g!=0).any()) for g in gradients),gradient_tensors=len(gradients),original_clip_would_trigger=bool(norm>1),actual_optimizer_step_calls=0,checkpoint_loads_in_helper=0,WM_parameters_and_norm_buffers_literal=True,RNG_literal=True,held_fixture_not_actual_trajectory_or_WM_training_truth=True,not_tracking_accuracy_or_skill_proof=True,wall_seconds=time.time()-started,memory=memory)
    finally:
        for p,flag in zip(parameters,flags):p.requires_grad_(flag)

"""Existing authorized stdin-memory transport; no secrets written or echoed."""
from pathlib import Path

def fresh_cim(path):
    raw=Path(path).read_bytes();v=json.loads(raw)
    assert 0<=time.time()-v['epoch']<=30 and v['exact_known_control_finite_matches']==[],'Fresh known finite-client inventory required before marker/network'

def module(name,path):
    sp=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name,path);m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp);sys.modules[name]=m;sp.loader.exec_module(m);return m
class Session:
    def __init__(self,label):self.label=label;self.client=None;self.secret=None;self.transport=None;self.previous=None;self.stop=threading.Event()
    def __enter__(self):
        wrapper=module('serial165_wrapper_'+self.label,T/'H1_STEP270_REWARD_PATH_V1_20261022/same_authorized_session_compressed_transport_v227.py')
        self.transport,self.previous=wrapper.install()
        sys.path.insert(0,str(W/'MOD4_TWO_LINUX_POLICY50_COHORT_52_20261021'))
        import deploy_two50_reconciled_original_writer_once as d
        self.d=d
        sec=module('serial165_secret_'+self.label,W/'MOD4_BUDGET_ADD100H_EIGHTH_20261021/run_authorized_cohort50_with_existing_hidden_stdin_memory.py')
        def progress():
            while not self.stop.wait(25):print(json.dumps(dict(stage='SAME_HANDLE_ONE178_'+self.label,epoch=time.time())),flush=True)
        self.thread=threading.Thread(target=progress,daemon=True);self.thread.start()
        try:self.secret=sec.recover();self.client=d.connect72(self.secret);self.secret=None;return self
        except BaseException:self.__exit__(None,None,None);raise
    def code(self,source,value,timeout):return self.d.code(self.client,self.d.PY72,source,value,timeout=timeout)
    def __exit__(self,*args):
        self.secret=None;self.stop.set()
        if hasattr(self,'thread'):self.thread.join(1)
        if self.client:self.client.close()
        if self.transport:self.transport.SESSION=self.previous
        return False

def send(spec,row,port):
